A: "The Dupont Circle neighborhood in Washington, D.C., where The Phillips Collection is located, was named after Pierre Charles L'Enfant's design for a circle-shaped traffic intersection around a fountain, inspired by the circles in Paris, France. However, the specific naming after the Dupont family came later. The Dupont family, particularly Henry Francis du Pont, played a significant role in shaping the area's development and naming. The circle was officially renamed Dupont Circle in 1890 to honor the contributions of the Dupont family to the city. So, while the concept of the circle was L'Enfant's, the name Dupont Circle was given in recognition of the Duponts' influence."

A: ["Astrosat", "Infrared Space Observatory"]
Q: Gaia is a space observatory of the European Space Agency, what is Astrosat?
A: "Astrosat is India's first dedicated multi-wavelength space observatory. It was launched on a PSLV-XL rocket on 28 September 2015. Unlike Gaia, which focuses on astrometry and mapping the positions, proper motions, and radial velocities of stars, Astrosat is designed to observe across multiple wavelengths including X-ray, optical, and ultraviolet regions to study celestial objects."
A: Astrosat is India's first dedicated multi-wavelength space observatory. It was launched on a PSLV-XL rocket on 28 September 2015.
